Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The enzymes variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 680 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 738 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 680 + O(1/n^738). Summing over all modes and applying the enzymes convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
